Motivation: 算法交易市场规模已超过200亿美元，信号鲁棒性的微小提升即可转化为显著的经济回报。然而，现有股票预测模型的评估在使用超参数选择时并未显式针对制度鲁棒性进行优化。这构成了方法论上的空白，本论文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 本研究对五类模型（包括表格深度学习架构如TabNet及梯度提升树如XGBoost）在约300只美国大盘股11年的日度观测数据上进行训练，采用贝叶斯优化配置超参数，优化目标为跨三种统计上不同的市场制度的交易表现。通过秩聚合方法将XGBoost与TabNet组合构建混合集成，并进行鲁棒性测试（输入噪声模拟）和因子分析（CAPM alpha与beta）。

Result: 制度鲁棒的超参数选择与样本外泛化正相关——信号精度在测试期四个季度均保持在随机基线之上，且组合表现在模拟输入噪声下缓慢退化直至越过阈值后崩塌。单一表格深度学习架构均未超越梯度提升树，但混合集成模型（XGBoost+TabNet经秩聚合）实现了年化收益51.26%、夏普比率2.44、CAPM alpha为0.423（p=0.011）的显著表现，近零beta表明收益来自选股而非市场敞口。另类数据在技术和基本面特征之后仅起次要作用，且在空头端贡献强于多头端，并因模型类别而异。

Conclusion: 研究确立了制度鲁棒性应成为股票预测模型超参数选择中的显式优化目标。混合集成策略展示了优于单一架构的潜力，而另类数据的边际价值有限且不对称。论文提供了一个可交互的应用以实时探索结果，实时数据集成是走向实际部署的最后一步。

Abstract: Algorithmic trading now represents a market exceeding $20 billion, where even marginal gains in signal robustness can translate into economically significant returns. Existing evaluations of equity prediction models do not explicitly target regime robustness during hyperparameter selection. Five model classes are trained on daily observations from approximately 300 large-cap US equities over eleven years, with Bayesian optimisation configured to target trading performance across three statistically different market regimes. Regime-robust hyperparameter selection is associated with out-of-sample generalisation, as signal precision remains above the random baseline across all four quarters of the test period, and portfolio performance slowly degrades under simulated input noise before collapsing beyond a defined threshold. No individual tabular deep learning architecture outperforms gradient-boosted trees, but combining XGBoost and TabNet using rank aggregation produces a Hybrid ensemble with an annualised return of 51.26%, a Sharpe ratio of 2.44, and a statistically significant CAPM alpha of 0.423 (p = 0.011). A near-zero beta indicates this outperformance is driven by stock selection, not market exposure. Alternative data plays a secondary role once technical and fundamental features are accounted for, as well as contributing more strongly on the short side than the long, and varies by model class. An interactive application makes these results explorable in real time, with live data integration the remaining step toward practical deployment.

</details>


### [272] [Cone Extended Rayleigh Quotients for Directed Graph Learning: Minimax Spectral Certificates, Sensitivity, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27122)
*Yavdat Sh. Il'yasov,Nur F. Valeev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向有向图学习的非对称传播算子谱认证、灵敏度分析与控制方法。基于双侧锥Rayleigh框架，该方法无需对称性、非负性或锥保持假设即可对广义束B_θ−λG进行谱上/下界包络，并提供光滑可微的软min/max替代以保持严格单侧界。通过Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C的灵敏度公式实现一阶最优图干预和自适应谱控制。在有向Cora引文网络上，该方法在0.5%累计边权削减预算下将区分谱水平降低约21.5%，且不损失测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图的传播算子是非对称的，具有不同的左右谱结构，传统谱方法通常要求对称性、非负性或锥保持等约束，限制了其在可训练图学习场景中的适用性。作者希望建立一套无需这些强假设的谱认证、灵敏度分析和控制方法论，以支持有向图上的可学习传播与自适应干预。

Method: 基于双侧锥Rayleigh框架处理广义束B_θ−λG，在正象限设定下推导可计算的上下锥界作为区分锥水平的后验包络；引入光滑的软min/max替代函数，在保证严格单侧界（具有显式逼近误差）的前提下保持对训练参数的可微性。对简单内层水平，推导出Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C形式的灵敏度公式，据此进行一阶最优图支持和自适应谱控制。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法可推广至非锥保持算子和有向学习设定。非对称扰动揭示了从内部特征对到边界互补准对（含非谱锥水平）的转变；对照实验显示对称化会移除由边方向携带的预测信息。在有向Cora数据集上，自适应重计算左右灵敏度在0.5%累计边权削减预算下将区分谱水平降低约21.5%，且所训练模型和测试数据的精度无变化。

Conclusion: 该工作为有向图学习中的非对称传播算子提供了统一的谱分析框架，兼具理论保证（严格的单侧界和显式误差）与实用性（可微性、一阶干预最优性）。实验结果证明该方法在超越传统锥保持假设的实际场景中同样有效，且谱灵敏度驱动的最小干预能显著调控谱特性而不损害下游任务性能。

Abstract: Directed graph learning naturally leads to trainable nonsymmetric propagation operators with distinct right and left spectral structures. Building on the two-sided cone Rayleigh framework for generalized pencils \[ B_θ-λG, \] we develop a learning-oriented methodology for spectral certification, sensitivity analysis, and control without requiring symmetry, nonnegativity, or cone preservation. In the positive-orthant setting, computable lower and upper cone bounds provide an a posteriori enclosure of a distinguished cone level, while smooth soft-min/max surrogates preserve rigorous one-sided bounds with explicit approximation errors and remain differentiable with respect to the trainable parameters.
  For a simple interior level, the right and left modes satisfy \[ Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C, \] yielding first-order optimal graph-supported interventions under prescribed perturbation budgets and motivating adaptive spectral control. Numerical experiments demonstrate the applicability of the approach beyond cone-preserving operators and in directed learning settings. Signed nonsymmetric perturbations reveal a transition from interior eigenpairs to boundary complementary quasi-pairs, including non-spectral cone levels, while controlled experiments show that symmetrization can remove predictive information carried solely by edge direction.
  On the directed Cora citation network, adaptive recomputation of the right--left sensitivity reduces the distinguished spectral level by approximately $21.5\%$ under a cumulative edge-weight reduction budget of $0.5\%$, with no observed change in test accuracy for the trained model and data split considered.

</details>


### [273] [TRACE-CRC: Trajectory-Adaptive Conformal Risk Control for Multi-Step Channel State Information Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27124)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Mehdi Sattari,Javad Aliakbari,Tommy Svensson,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-CRC方法，用于多步CSI预测中的轨迹感知不确定性量化。该方法通过Frobenius范数不确定性球、未来步误差画像、轨迹难度分层以及LTT风险控制，确保整个预测轨迹中至少有一个未来帧被覆盖的风险得到控制，同时保持不确定性球体较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习CSI预测器仅提供点预测，缺乏校准的不确定性估计。在多步CSI预测中，下游决策（如波束成形或调度）可能因预测轨迹的任何部分不可靠而失败，因此需要对整个轨迹进行不确定性量化，而不仅仅是单步校准。

Method: TRACE-CRC结合了三种关键组件：(1) 未来步依赖的误差画像（error profiling），刻画不同未来时刻的预测误差分布；(2) 轨迹难度分层（trajectory difficulty stratification），根据轨迹难度进行分层校准；(3) 学习-测试（LTT）风险控制，利用共形风险控制（CRC）框架控制"至少一个未来帧未被覆盖"的轨迹级风险。同时构建Frobenius范数不确定性球包围预测CSI矩阵。

Result: 实验表明，TRACE-CRC实现了可靠的轨迹级覆盖率，其不确定性球明显小于保守的多步校正方法，同时避免了紧凑的逐步法（stepwise）和自适应共形基线（adaptive conformal baselines）存在的轨迹欠覆盖问题。

Conclusion: TRACE-CRC为多步CSI预测提供了一种有效的轨迹级不确定性量化方案，在覆盖率和不确定性球体大小之间取得了良好平衡，克服了传统逐步校准和保守校正方法的局限。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of time-varying channel state information (CSI) is essential for efficient wireless communication. Each CSI frame is a matrix-valued representation of the wireless channel response, and a sequence of CSI frames forms a temporal channel trajectory. Modern deep learning-based CSI predictors, however, often provide only point predictions and lack calibrated uncertainty estimates. This limitation is particularly problematic in multi-step CSI prediction, where the target is a sequence of future CSI matrices, and downstream decisions such as beamforming or scheduling may fail if any part of the predicted trajectory is unreliable. We propose trajectory-adaptive calibration and error profiling with conformal risk control (TRACE-CRC), a method for trajectory-aware uncertainty quantification in multi-step CSI prediction. TRACE-CRC constructs Frobenius-norm uncertainty balls around predicted CSI matrices and controls the risk that at least one future frame is uncovered. Instead of calibrating each future step independently, TRACE-CRC combines future-step-dependent error profiling, trajectory difficulty stratification, and learn-then-test (LTT) risk control. Empirically, TRACE-CRC achieves reliable trajectory-level coverage with substantially smaller uncertainty balls than conservative multi-step corrections, while avoiding the trajectory undercoverage of compact stepwise and adaptive conformal baselines.

</details>


### [274] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RSS（接收信号强度）的新型低功耗定位方法，通过旋转高增益发射机和概率建模推断到达角（AoA），利用高斯过程和双重随机变分推断重建移动接收器的运动轨迹，实现约15m精度（38mg、<180uW）和约10m精度（<600uW）的跟踪，并成功应用于熊蜂归巢飞行追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多领域（如运动生态学、物联网、机器人）需要定位过小且功耗受限而无法实现GNSS的设备。现有基于RSS推断AoA的低功耗方法受限于测距能力和大量RSS测量带来的功耗需求，亟需一种既轻量又低功耗、且在复杂地形中具有足够精度和范围的定位方案。

Method: 采用旋转高增益发射机在300m范围内以最少的RSS测量实现信号采集，通过概率建模从RSS中推断AoA；接收器运动路径使用高斯过程建模，并借助双重随机变分推断进行路径重建。

Result: 在38mg（含电源）超轻接收器上实现约15m跟踪精度（<180uW功耗），通过增加RSS测量可将精度提升至约10m（<600uW功耗），并成功演示了熊蜂（Bombus terrestris）归巢飞行追踪。

Conclusion: 该方法为无法使用GNSS的超轻量、低功耗移动接收器提供了一种可扩展且实用的定位方案，有望支持飞行昆虫行为学等领域的相关研究。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [275] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用图神经网络（GNN）解决归纳式相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering）问题，这是对传统相关聚类（CC）的推广，旨在处理未见过的图实例。该方法通过在学习阶段挖掘常见结构模式和节点特征，使框架能够以极低的计算开销泛化到同一分布的新图，推理时间可降低高达5个数量级，同时近似比保持在最优基线解的10%以内，并在标准传导式CC基准上取得竞争性结果。此外，该框架还可用作可学习的图分类池化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相关聚类算法存在两个核心缺陷：(1) 大规模可扩展性问题严重；(2) 本质上是传导式的（transductive），即面对任何新的问题实例都必须从头运行算法。这些局限严重阻碍了CC在现实场景中的部署与应用，因此需要一种能够处理未见图实例的归纳式（inductive）求解框架。

Method: 利用图神经网络（GNN）构建归纳式相关聚类框架。该框架在训练阶段学习利用图数据中的常见结构模式和节点特征信息，从而能够对新图（来自同分布）进行高效推理，而无需重新执行完整的聚类优化算法。同时，该方法还被设计为一种可学习的池化机制，用于图分类任务中以捕获网络的层级结构信息。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 在归纳式设置下，推理时间降低高达5个数量级，近似比保持在最优基线解约10%以内；(2) 在标准传导式CC基准上也取得了具有竞争力的结果；(3) 作为图分类的池化层时，能有效增强GNN捕获层级结构信息的能力，验证了其实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文成功将GNN引入相关聚类问题，实现了从传导式到归纳式的跨越，在保持良好近似质量的同时大幅提升了推理效率。该方法不仅解决了CC的可扩展性问题，还拓展了其应用场景（如作为可学习池化机制），展示了GNN在组合优化与图学习交叉领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [276] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PDPO框架，利用扩散策略生成短期动作块进行机器人人群导航，通过离线预训练+在线PPO微调，并在边界约束下取得了优于强基线的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在人群导航中每个时间步只输出单一反应性动作，难以表征多样化的短期避障策略，限制了在密集、动态、多模态人机交互下的安全高效决策能力。

Method: PDPO框架包含三个关键部分：(1)离线阶段利用碰撞避让演示对扩散策略进行预训练；(2)在线阶段将去噪过程视为内部决策过程，使用PPO进行微调；(3)执行阶段生成五步动作块并以后退时域(receding-horizon)方式应用。同时引入将边界违规视为碰撞的新评测设置。

Result: PDPO在成功率上优于强基线方法，消融实验表明动作块在改进的有界(bounded)基准中尤其重要。

Conclusion: 扩散策略结合动作块生成能够提升人群导航的性能，且边界约束的设置有助于发现并解决评测中的伪影问题。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [277] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceBench，一个基于仿真的框架，用于生成受控的根因归因任务，评估LLM代理在时间序列异常检测与根因分析上的表现，并揭示其行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理已被广泛应用于真实系统时间序列的异常检测与根因分析，但在受控条件下的系统性评估尚属空白。缺乏受控基准使得无法严格评估这些代理的能力与行为模式。

Method: 构建TraceBench仿真框架，通过模拟物理动力学系统生成受控的根因归因任务；每个任务中代理需判断系统参数是否被改变以及哪个参数被改变。从三个可解释的机械系统中生成任务，并在受控实验条件下系统评估四个LLM代理。

Result: 代理从领域上下文中获益显著，主要通过数值控制台输出而非可视化来探索数据；当需要生成Python脚本为每个时间序列样本预测根因标签时，代理的表现普遍差于直接提交预测。

Conclusion: TraceBench为LLM代理的根因分析能力提供了首个受控评估框架，揭示了领域上下文、数据探索方式和输出形式对代理性能的重要影响，并公开了数据集、轨迹、结果与排行榜。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks. In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [278] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控对比框架，用于在演化交互图场景下估计同伴效应，并开发了动态网络双重稳健估计器（DynaNet-DR），在多种假设下具有双重稳健性，且在半合成基准上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当交互图随时间演化时，同伴效应的估计变得困难，因为预分配网络历史、动态同伴暴露和后分配网络变化具有不同的因果作用，传统的调解分解方法难以适用。

Method: 引入受控对比框架，将潜在结果依自身处理、时间聚合的同伴暴露和后分配网络演化汇总进行索引；定义自身处理、同伴暴露、受控网络演化和联合受控对比；开发DynaNet-DR估计器，结合时间分解倾向得分与归一化增广，并加入代表性分数预测、固定截断和有限样本稳定化。

Result: 在一致性、汇总充分性、顺序可交换性、正性、烦扰函数收敛和弱依赖等假设下，规范估计器在结果回归或倾向得分估计量之一一致时具有一致性；在固定真实时序图序列的半合成基准中，针对完整画像的方法取得了更优的估计精度。

Conclusion: 半合成基准评估了汇总索引对比而非反事实边生成；MathOverflow研究是在给定假设下的一项观察性说明。框架提供了一种不依赖调解分解的方式来刻画演化网络中的同伴效应。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [279] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明结构化支持向量机（SSVM）的Fisher一致性必要条件是任务损失为满足所有输出三元组存在公共测地点的度量，但该条件对坐标式argmax解码器并非充分。论文完全分类了正加权树度量下的argmax一致性（仅当树是路径时成立），并提供了全支持分布下的反例（5个输出即可构造，最小为K_{2,3}，以及三维Hamming立方体），所有最优性声明均有精确的原对偶证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化预测中，SSVM的Fisher一致性已知需要一个必要测度条件（任务损失为度量且输出三元组有公共测地点）。然而这一条件是否足以保证坐标式argmax解码器的一致性尚不明确。作者旨在揭示该条件并非充分，并系统刻画在哪些度量/结构下argmax一致性可以成立。

Method: 通过构造反例来推翻必要条件的充分性，采用极小反例分析（寻找最小的输出规模使不一致性出现）；对正加权树度量作完全分类，运用原对偶（primal-dual）证书精确验证最优性声明；分别考察边界分布与全支持分布下的一致性与否。

Result: （1）四输出单位星图存在精确最优评分向量，其所有最大元均严格非Bayes，四个输出在满足条件的度量中是最小的；（2）正加权树度量中argmax一致性成立当且仅当树为路径；（3）分支树上的失败仅限于边界分布，所有树的满支持分布均保持argmax性质；（4）在满足公共测地点条件的度量中，五个输出对于满支持反例是必要且充分的，K_{2,3}是无穷K_{m,n}族的最小成员；（5）三维Hamming立方体存在满支持反例。

Conclusion: 在结构化SVM的多面体设置中，存在一种"解码器间隙"：即使嵌入能保证校准链接的存在性，也不能确保任意指定的argmax链接在每一个替代风险最小化器上都被验证为一致的。现有必要条件不足以支撑坐标式argmax解码器的一致性保证。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [280] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对冬小麦氮肥施用量推荐问题，提出以利润损失（而非预测精度）来评估机器学习模型的性能。在892条实测产量响应曲线上进行测试，发现机器学习作为独立预测器效果不佳，但作为标准建议的"修正器"（correction）能显著减少利润损失，且兼具减排定价价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的冬小麦氮肥施用量建议是在季节前设定，未考虑价格波动，而近年价格大幅波动使得最优施氮量变动可达每公顷数十公斤。机器学习常被提出作为解决方案，但通常仅以预测精度评估，而高预测精度并不必然带来更高的施氮利润。因此需要一种直接以利润为评价指标的方法。

Method: 构建基于892条实测产量响应曲线的测试平台，扫掠氮肥与谷物价格比以覆盖所有价格情景。将机器学习模型的推荐施氮量直接以利润损失（forgone profit）进行评分，并与标准UK建议比较。进一步提出一种简单的"修正步骤"（correction step）应用于模型输出之后，并测试其跨站点泛化能力。

Result: 机器学习作为独立预测器失败，没有任何模型能在农场容忍度内恢复最优施氮量，且基准噪声表明任何模型都无法做到。在正常价格下，所有模型在利润上均输于标准建议。但简单修正步骤可将利润损失削减四分之一，且无需再训练的固定修正可在第二站点减少43%的损失。混合方法（标准建议+阻尼修正）消除了偏差并减少了罕见的大额损失。

Conclusion: 机器学习的价值不在于替代标准建议，而在于作为利润评分的修正器补充标准建议。此外，相同的价格扫描可为排放削减定价，其成本与当前碳价相当，表明该方法具备环境政策层面的衍生价值。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [281] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO，一个面向IMU传感器数据的人类活动识别（HAR）领域基础模型，通过两阶段训练框架（异构感知自监督预训练 + 同义词感知软对比学习的语言对齐）同时解决传感异构性和开放集泛化问题。在7个留出数据集上超越5个SOTA基线，以约3500万参数（比MOMENT少10倍）实现零样本开放集准确率提升13.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR领域缺乏一个能够跨受试者、设备和活动泛化的统一模型，主要面临两大挑战：一是传感异构性（采样率、通道配置、传感器位置不同），二是对未见活动和标签词汇的泛化能力差。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：第一阶段通过异构感知自监督学习预训练IMU编码器，包含自适应池化分词（adaptive-pooling tokenization）、通道独立特征提取以及将自然语言传感器描述注入每个通道嵌入的上下文传感器条件化；第二阶段通过同义词感知软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，利用余弦相似度检索实现开放集识别，无需为每个数据集单独训练分类器。

Result: 在10个公共HAR数据集上训练、7个留出数据集上评估，HALO在全部8个聚合指标上超越5个SOTA基线；在基线匹配输入条件下仍在4个设置中领先3个。仅用约3500万可训练参数（比MOMENT的3.412亿少10倍），零样本开放集准确率较最新基础模型MOMENT提升13.7个百分点。但在2个存在严重分布偏移的数据集上，包括HALO在内的所有模型零样本性能均崩溃。

Conclusion: HALO证明了领域特定的轻量级IMU基础模型能够在保持强开放集泛化能力的同时高效处理传感异构性，但严重分布偏移仍是开放挑战，未来需要进一步研究如何增强模型在极端偏移场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [282] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个重要性评分指标，用于解释多头Transformer在表格数据学习中的注意力头行为。基于40个多样化的表格数据集实验，发现按重要性评分由低到高逐步删除注意力头时模型最鲁棒（72.5%的实验案例中表现最佳），而先删除最重要的头会导致性能最大下降。重要头分散在六个注意力层中，无明显层内规律，且与图像/语言领域不同，表格数据中注意力头的重要性因数据集架构和特征空间不同而有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算密集且不透明的深度学习模型可以通过分析其数据变换方式来更好地理解和优化。虽然深度Transformer在计算机视觉和自然语言处理中已被广泛研究，但它们在表格数据上的应用仍相对未被充分探索，缺乏针对表格数据的注意力头解释性度量方法。

Method: 提出一种重要性评分指标来评估多头Transformer中每个注意力头对表格数据学习的贡献。在40个不同的表格数据集上进行实验，通过从低到高和从高到低逐步删除注意力头来测试模型的鲁棒性，并分析六个注意力层中每个头的重要性分数分布。

Result: 在72.5%的实验案例中，按最低重要性分数优先删除注意力头时模型最鲁棒；删除最重要头部导致分类性能下降最大。重要头分散在各层之间，无一致的层内趋势。与图像和语言域不同，表格数据集中注意力头的重要性在不同数据集的模式和特征空间之间差异显著。

Conclusion: 提出的重要性评分可以提升Transformer架构的效率和冗余度，并已公开测量单个注意力头重要性的源代码，为表格数据领域中Transformer模型的可解释性研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [283] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"电路冷凝"(Circuit Condensation)方法，通过后训练模型将行为浓缩到更小的因果图中：每轮剪除低归因边并用低秩适配器补偿，从而得到较冻结基线平均小8.1倍、最高达316倍的冷凝电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性中的电路发现往往返回数百条边，难以逐一检验、比较和穷举验证。冻结式电路发现方法产出的电路过大，亟需一种能压缩电路规模的方法来提升可解释性和可验证性。

Method: 每轮迭代执行两步：(1) 剪除低归因边；(2) 训练一个低秩适配器，通过保留的边匹配原始模型输出。仅在任务性能和通用能力不受损时才保留剪除结果。该方法将权值更新与搜索结合而非仅为搜索。

Result: 在4种行为、8个模型上，冷凝电路在32个设置中的30个优于最强冻结基线，平均缩小8.1倍、最大316倍。去除权值更新后，29/32设置电路变大，证明权值更新是关键。19条电路的子集测试中11条不可再压缩。间接宾语识别任务中，冷凝仅需24个头（17个有文献记录的角色），而冻结基线需61个头（36个无记录）。

Conclusion: 电路冷凝能产出充分且可追溯的电路子集，而非对已发布机制的简单重建；所得电路能追踪原始模型的下一token分布并预测其错误，且边与边之间存在依赖关系，不能孤立理解。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [284] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在演化算子网络(LEON)，将世界动作模型(WAM)中的潜在状态转移从Transformer风格的token交互架构改为基于受控Koopman生成器视角的算子传播与加性强制机制，验证了转移实现作为独立于预测表示和策略耦合的架构选择的重要性，并在闭环性能与鲁棒性上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在WAM使用Transformer预测器实现潜在转移，其归纳结构侧重于token交互而非时间演化，缺乏与系统动力学演化性质相匹配的结构性先验。作者认为转移实现应被视为一个独立于预测表示和预测-策略耦合的架构选择，值得系统研究。

Method: 提出LEON(Latent Evolution Operator Network)，在学习到的可观测空间中通过上下文调制的算子传播和加性强制来建模潜在演化，理论依据是受控Koopman生成器视角。具体而言，LEON将依赖上下文的转移变化组织在共享的演化算子结构周围，同时保留一条补充的加性变化路径。作者在受控动力学系统上验证演化特定的归纳偏置及算子传播与强制力的互补作用，并在两种不同的WAM公式中评估。

Result: 受控动力学系统实验验证了LEON引入的演化特定归纳偏置以及算子传播与加性强制的互补角色。在两种将潜在预测以不同方式集成到策略中的WAM公式下，LEON在闭环性能和鲁棒性上均优于基线，并在完全替换转移模块时依然有效。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了转移实现在潜在WAM中是一个具有实质影响的架构选择，LEON通过算子化的演化建模为潜在世界模型的转移设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [285] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MM-Spectrum，一个稀疏Mixture-of-Experts框架，用于从多模态多光谱数据推断分子结构，通过模态感知路由机制和共享/交互专家解决多模态不平衡导致的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多模态光谱测量推断分子结构需要整合互补但高度异质的信号，但直接拼接多光谱序列的常见范式会因模态间异质性和多模态不平衡而出现异常的性能退化。

Method: MM-Spectrum框架包含：1) 显式的模态感知路由机制，将光谱身份信息暴露给路由器；2) 共享专家和交互专家，并配备异构专家容量，以提取模态特有信息和跨模态协同信息，同时抑制噪声干扰。

Result: 在完整模态、双模态和缺失模态设置下的分子结构解析任务中，MM-Spectrum实现了一致且显著的改进，并通过消融研究和可解释性分析加以验证。

Conclusion: MM-Spectrum有效解决了多模态多光谱条件下模态不平衡导致的性能下降问题，验证了显式模态感知路由和异构专家设计在光谱-结构解析中的有效性。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [286] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuantumBoostNet是一个混合经典-量子架构，用于心脏超声（超声心动图）视图/角度识别任务。该模型结合经典骨干网络与两个输出头（经典头和量子头），其中量子头由10量子比特参数化量子电路实现。通过两阶段训练和自适应混合参数切换机制，模型在心脏超声视图识别上超越现有最先进模型，并在通用图像分类基准上表现优异且对噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声视图的准确识别是心血管成像的关键步骤，对精确解剖解释、可靠测量和减少临床错误至关重要。尽管计算机视觉显著进步，但现有最先进模型在专用医学影像任务上因数据噪声水平高而表现欠佳，亟需一种能在此类噪声环境中保持性能的新架构。

Method: 提出QuantumBoostNet混合经典-量子架构：以经典骨干网络为基础，配备经典和量子两个输出头；量子头采用参数化10量子比特量子电路实现。训练分两个阶段进行，通过监控损失动态的混合参数控制两个头之间的自适应切换。

Result: 大量实验表明，即使可模拟的量子比特数量有限，QuantumBoostNet在心脏超声视图识别任务上仍一致性地超越现有最先进的经典模型和混合经典-量子模型，相对最优竞争对手取得性能提升。此外，该模型在既有图像分类基准上表现优异，并对噪声展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持继续开发面向专业医学影像应用的混合经典-量子模型，表明此类架构在噪声较高的医学数据场景中具有潜力。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [287] [Beyond Parallel Blindness: Information Floors and Model Gaps in Block Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27339)
*Xinwei Qiang,Xiang Fang,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"信息下限"（information floor）概念，将块草拟者（block drafters）被拒绝的损失分离为两部分：缺少块内路径信息与对可观测信息的建模不完善。通过在四个领域、四个开源目标和一个前沿API目标上进行评估，发现三方面结果：①全并行信息下限在最终槽位可达0.286，将最佳提案的逐槽接受率限制在71%；②仅提供一个已实现token即可消除86%–100%的信息下限，显示出块内路径信息的短程局部性；③现有草拟者仍远高于其信息下限，模型差距占Dflash拒绝的43%–64%和DSpark预言机条件拒绝的85%–92%。这些发现将短程条件化的价值与提案质量分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块草拟方法在一次前向传播中提出多个token，但被接受前的拒绝混合了两种不同的损失来源：块内路径信息缺失（即在早期目标token实现前无法获得路径信息）和模型对可观察信息的建模不完善。现有的"接受长度"指标无法区分这两种损失，阻碍了对草拟者对提案质量与条件化结构价值的系统评估。

Method: 提出"信息下限"（information floor）概念，定义为在指定条件化顺序下的最小期望拒绝率；超出此下限的拒绝称为"模型差距"（model gap）。两者从目标rollout中估计，并在四个领域、四种开放权重目标和一个前沿API目标上系统评估。

Result: 研究发现三方面结果：①全并行场景下，Qwen3-4B最终槽位的信息下限达到0.286，将最佳提案的每槽位接受率上限定为71%；②仅实现一个token即可消除86%–100%的信息下限，且独立互信息分析也验证了这一短程局部性；③现有草拟者仍远高于下限——最终槽位的模型差距占DFlash拒绝的43%–64%，占DSpark预言机条件拒绝的85%–92%。

Conclusion: 本文成功将块草拟中的两类拒绝损失分离，量化了短程条件化信息的价值与提案模型质量各自的贡献。核心结论是：一个已实现token就足以消除绝大部分信息下限，说明块内路径信息的价值高度局部化；而当前主流草拟者（如DFlash和DSpark）的拒绝主要来源于模型差距而非信息下限，尤其是在DSpark的预言机条件下。这为改进块草拟提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Block drafters propose several tokens in one forward pass, before earlier target tokens are realised. Their rejection mixes two losses: missing within-block path information and imperfect modelling of observable information. Accepted length cannot distinguish them. We separate the two with an information floor, the minimum expected rejection at a specified conditioning order; rejection above this floor is the model gap. Estimating both from target rollouts across four domains, four open-weight targets, and a frontier API target yields three findings. First, the all-parallel floor reaches $0.286$ at the final slot on Qwen3-4B, limiting even the best proposal to $71\%$ per-slot acceptance. Second, one realised token removes $86$--$100\%$ of this floor, a locality also recovered by an independent mutual-information analysis. Third, current drafters remain far above their floors: the final-slot model gap accounts for $43$--$64\%$ of DFlash rejection and $85$--$92\%$ of DSpark's oracle-conditioned rejection. These findings separate the value of short-range conditioning from proposal quality.

</details>


### [288] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略（ES）作为LLM推理后训练范式的优化行为和机制，发现ES相比GRPO能产生更广泛的推理覆盖，理论上和实证上均表现出更高的Pass@K性能，同时揭示了ES的参数漂移具有功能稀疏性，并提出GRPO-ES混合训练策略以及针对大模型需更小种群规模的超参数设计发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES虽然近期作为记忆高效的LLM推理后训练范式出现，但其优化行为尚未被充分研究，导致难以界定其相比主流后训练范式（如GRPO）的优势范围。作者希望填补这一空白，理解ES的动力学和机制。

Method: 通过系统研究ES的动力学和机制：(1)从理论上证明ES种群中verifier投影的Jensen-Shannon多样性与更高Pass@K性能相关；(2)通过实证对比ES与GRPO的熵变化、Pass@1和Pass@K指标；(3)开发顺序GRPO-ES训练策略；(4)分析ES中参数漂移的功能稀疏性；(5)研究超参数（如种群大小）在不同规模LLM下的影响。

Result: 实证显示ES比GRPO获得更广泛推理覆盖，GRPO表现熵塌缩而ES在提升Pass@1的同时获得更高Pass@K；ES的性能提升仅由稀疏子集的较大幅度更新贡献，且不必然导致灾难性遗忘；更大LLM需要更小种群规模。

Conclusion: ES应被视为一种独特的推理后训练范式，而非GRPO的次优记忆高效替代方案。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EduRiskX，一个结合时间Transformer预测器与F-Logic符号推理的神经符号框架，用于在线教育学生学业风险预测。在OULAD数据集上达到0.900准确率和0.894的F1分数，平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率达94.30%，效果优于PatchTST、iTransformer、LSTM和CNN等基线模型，同时通过F-Logic规则提供可解释的预测依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线教育学生学业风险预测模型存在两大问题：早期检测能力有限，且可解释性不足，导致"黑箱"信任危机，阻碍了模型在实际教学场景中的应用落地。需要一种既能提供早期预警又能给出可理解解释的框架。

Method: EduRiskX采用神经符号混合架构：(1) 神经组件——基于时间Transformer的预测器，利用时间注意力建模学生纵向活动序列，结合类别加权损失和动态周截断；(2) 符号组件——基于F-Logic规则库构建数据驱动的专家系统，规则源自教育理论（参与理论与学生整合模型），仅从训练数据中构造，模拟人类教育者的诊断逻辑；(3) 融合机制——通过逻辑回归将神经风险概率与符号置信度得分进行融合，学习两个信号各自的相对贡献。

Result: 在OULAD数据集上采用严格的80/10/10学生级划分，学期末（第38周）准确率为0.900，F1分数为0.894，平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率达94.30%。与PatchTST、iTransformer等最新时间序列模型及LSTM、CNN等常见深度学习基线相比，在相同条件下获得更高的召回率和更早的风险识别。

Conclusion: EduRiskX不仅提升了学业风险预测的性能和早期检测能力，还通过F-Logic模块提供结构化规则解释，将预测结果与可观察的行为模式及教育理论联系起来，解决了黑箱信任问题，为在线教育中的及时干预提供了可靠且可信赖的工具。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [290] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了独立LLM与多步骤agentic管线在ICU死亡风险预测解释中的表现。XGBoost模型AUROC为0.855。agentic管线在消除结果泄露、循证依据和数值特异性上优于独立LLM，但SHAP对齐和方向一致性较低，提示两者应结合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测ICU死亡率的机器学习模型很准确，但特征归因方法本身难以提供临床可用的叙事解释。大语言模型有望弥合这一差距，而多步骤agentic管线通过分离数据解释、指南核查和最终解释，可能提供更安全、更具体的临床解释。本研究旨在在保留原始对比设计的基础上，使主要临床发现更加明确。

Method: 使用本地eICU Demo数据集（2,353例ICU住院，死亡率8.1%），训练XGBoost模型预测ICU死亡率。随后在分层抽样的38例解释子集上，对比独立LLM与四步骤agentic管线的解释质量。评估指标包括结果泄露、SHAP对齐（Jaccard相似度）、方向一致性、指南依据、数值特异性和临床合理性。

Result: XGBoost的AUROC为0.855（95% CI 0.796-0.906），AUPRC为0.332。独立LLM产生1例显式结果泄露，agentic管线为0例。在14例重叠样本中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）更高；而agentic管线的指南依据（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和合理性（0.700 vs 0.671）更好。

Conclusion: Agentic分解可能提升与安全相关的依据基础及患者特异性细节，但在高风险的临床风险解释场景中，应搭配基于归因的检查（如SHAP）使用，以兼顾解释的可信度和临床实用性。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [291] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个关于大模型（LMs）在电池预测与健康管理（BPHM）中应用的综合性综述，系统梳理了基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练的大模型如何解决传统BPHM方法在计算效率、跨域泛化、数据依赖和可解释性方面的瓶颈，并提出了面向数据生态、工业验证、可信度和端侧部署的未来研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法（如物理模型和任务导向的深度学习方法）面临计算效率低、参数化困难、跨域泛化能力不足、依赖大量标记run-to-failure数据以及模型可解释性差等长期瓶颈。大模型基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练，提供了一种变革性的新范式来克服这些挑战，但此前缺乏对该领域系统性综述。

Method: 本文首先阐明支撑大模型的基础技术，包括Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集和参数高效微调（PEFT）技术。随后从四个关键维度对最新进展进行分类：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、融合领域知识提升可解释性、实现系统级自动化。

Result: 综述显示大模型在BPHM各维度均展现出有前景的结果，但仍面临数据可获取性、智能验证、可信度和部署可行性方面的重大挑战。文章提出了构建协作数据生态系统、验证工业应用智能、增强基于物理信息的可信设计以及实现高效端侧部署的路线图。

Conclusion: 本文建立了系统化理解大模型驱动BPHM的框架，为研究人员和从业者开发下一代电池管理系统提供了关键洞见，以支持电池全生命周期内的安全、可靠和自主运行。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [292] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PICasso，一个从自然语言规格自动生成、验证和优化光子集成电路（PIC）的AI辅助框架，并引入PIC-Set基准（36个参数化PIC设计任务）。PICasso结合结构化NL->YAML->GDS生成流水线、PDK感知知识注入、自动布局布线、DRC/LVS验证和SAX光子仿真。实验表明，相比纯LLM生成，PICasso在结构Spec@3上达92.7%，功能Spec@3达52%，并将电路插入损耗从4.98dB降至3.25dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子集成电路（PIC）设计高度依赖专家经验和手工流程，传统GUI工作流耗时且容易出错。尽管大语言模型（LLM）在代码生成等领域表现出色，但直接用于PIC设计时缺乏领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈，生成的netlist往往不可制造或功能不正确。作者希望将LLM从脆弱的netlist生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理。

Method: PICasso框架包含：(1) 结构化的NL->YAML->GDS生成流水线，将自然语言规格转化为结构化的YAML中间表示再生成GDS版图；(2) PDK感知知识注入，确保设计符合工艺设计套件约束；(3) 自动布局布线和DRC/LVS验证确保可制造性；(4) SAX光子仿真进行功能验证和优化。同时提出PIC-Set基准（36个参数化PIC设计任务）和统一评估协议，包括结构/功能Spec@k、优化效率和鲁棒性等新指标。

Result: 在高复杂度电路上，结构Spec@3达到92.7%，功能Spec@3达到52%，显著超过vanilla LLM生成。通过仿真引导优化，PICasso将电路平均插入损耗从4.98dB降至3.25dB（改善1.74dB），且运行时间与手工GUI工作流相当。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈的结合，能够将LLM从脆弱的netlist生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理，可生成可制造的版图且运行效率具有竞争力，为AI驱动的光子设计自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [293] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CIFQA，一个确定性工具支撑的多智能体LLM框架，用于计算密集型金融问答，通过将语言理解与数值执行分离，在定期存款查询基准上达到95.54%的计算密集型准确率，并证明架构设计比模型规模对数值可靠性更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算密集型的金融问答需要对结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和规则约束进行精确推理。尽管LLM在自然语言任务上表现优异，但在多步金融计算中常常产生数值错误但看似合理的答案，需要解决这一数值可靠性问题。

Method: CIFQA采用确定性的工具支撑多智能体架构，将语言理解与数值执行分离。框架分配了五个专门智能体：查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，而确定性的基于Python的工具负责金融计算和规则应用。针对定期存款查询场景进行了实例化。

Result: 在精选的定期存款查询基准上，CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%准确率，整体准确率90.87%，显著优于直接使用LLM的基线（即使基线上提供了完整公式、利率表和基准指令）。消融研究显示精确利率查找、期限计算、滚动年份调整和提前支取逻辑等确定性组件是关键贡献因素。17B开源模型在CIFQA框架下优于更大的前沿模型。

Conclusion: CIFQA证明了在金融计算任务中，架构设计比模型规模对数值可靠性更为重要，该框架虽在定期存款查询上验证，但可推广到更广泛的计算密集型金融推理任务。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [294] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于自生成强化学习（autotelic RL）的闭环框架CARL，用于探索和控制Lenia连续元胞自动机中的自组织涌现现象。CARL通过让智能体自主采样多样化目标并学习目标条件化策略，以最小局部扰动干预系统，实现了三个能力：发现稳定孤立子、控制已有孤立子的运动方向，以及通过高层级指令在实时引导孤立子穿越迷宫，且训练后的策略可零样本泛化到分布外条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元胞自动机及其他复杂系统探索方法大多以开环方式运行：设定初始条件、执行完整模拟、观察结果，过程中不进行任何干预。这种方式限制了研究人员对涌现现象的理解和控制能力，无法主动引导系统演化或实时操纵复杂系统中的自组织模式。

Method: 引入基于自生成强化学习的闭环框架：智能体自主采样多样化的目标，学习一个目标条件化策略，通过最小、局部的扰动来干预复杂系统的运行过程。将该框架实例化在Lenia（一种已知具有类生命自组织图案的连续元胞自动机）上，构建了名为CARL的智能体系统。

Result: CARL在广泛的Lenia更新规则下以比启发式基线更高的效率发现稳定的孤立子；能够通过少量干预学习控制已有孤立子的运动方向，证明其不仅能创造自组织模式，还能对其进行控制；人类可以通过训练后的智能体，输入高层级方向指令，在实时中引导孤立子穿越迷宫环境。跨多样化目标、更新规则和随机初始状态训练的智能体策略可零样本泛化到各种分布外条件。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明了一条通往人工实验者智能体的路径：这类智能体能够在自主或人类指导下，发现并控制复杂系统中的涌现现象，对复杂系统的探索方法从被动观测转向主动干预。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [295] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出能量预测中的"精度-效率悖论"：高精度预测模型在边缘环境中可能反而导致净能量亏损，并提出了一个考虑推理能耗与电池老化的总拥有成本（TCO）框架来最小化净能量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量预测的目标是最大化精度以减少能源浪费，但在军事等关键边缘环境中，高精度的复杂模型本身在推理时消耗大量能量，且加速电池老化，导致"精度越高、净能量损失越大"的悖论。现有方法忽视了推理能耗和电池退化带来的总能量损失。

Method: 提出一个总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一定义为能量损失形式（因为电池退化物化上意味着系统未来储能能力的耗散），以此作为评估和优化能量预测模型的统一度量标准。

Result: 在热敏感的边缘环境中，复杂架构因其高精度所节省的能量，往往被其高运行强度所导致的总能量损失所抵消甚至超过，验证了精度-效率悖论的存在。

Conclusion: 能量预测模型的设计不应仅以预测精度为目标，而应以最小化净能量损失（TCO）为目标，特别是在热敏感、资源受限的军事级边缘环境中，需要在精度与推理能耗、电池寿命之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>


### [296] [LLMs for Academic Workflows: An Evaluation of Literature Reviews Generated with Short and Long Context Windows of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26145)
*Muhammad Ali Chaudhry,Xinyuan Hao,Haifa Alwahaby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型在短上下文和长上下文设置下生成的文献综述质量，发现AI生成的综述需要人工监督才能达到学术发表标准；增加上下文窗口虽能纳入更广泛信息并保持连贯性，但也会加剧内容重复、关键工作遗漏和重描述轻综合等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究上下文窗口大小对AI生成文献综述质量的影响，以及AI在文献综述写作中的辅助作用，以明确AI生成综述的局限性及其与人类专家协作的边界。

Method: 基于Semantic Scholar和Arxiv的研究来源，生成了20篇AI文献综述，由两名研究人员在15个维度上进行评估，比较短上下文与长上下文设置下生成综述的质量差异。

Result: AI生成的文献综述需要人工监督才能达到学术出版标准；更大的上下文窗口使LLM能整合更广泛信息并保持长输入连贯性，但同时加剧了内容重复、关键工作遗漏以及倾向描述性而非综合性等问题。

Conclusion: AI生成的综述可提供基础性概述，但其产出必须经过领域专家的批判性评估和精炼。未来研究应考虑集成其他LLM和不同领域的微调模型，采用结合人类专业知识和AI能力的混合方法来克服本研究发现的问题。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [297] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系超图变换器（RHT），一种统一架构，将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入（PentE），并执行稀疏关系注意力（复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数的平方）。在Synthea合成电子健康记录数据集上，通过SNOMED CT条件码的多标签预测任务进行验证，RHT在嵌入语义一致性上表现最佳，而XGBoost在稀有码召回率上最优，同时RHT保持计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习（multi-table learning）在医疗保健等领域仍是一个重大挑战。关系数据融合了多种复杂性来源，包括大数据量、高维变量、高基数分类特征、复杂的表间依赖以及重复的时间观测。现有方法难以同时处理这些挑战，需要一种统一的架构来高效建模关系数据库中的复杂结构。

Method: RHT将关系数据库表示为超图（hypergraph），引入五维嵌入（PentE）来编码实体间的复杂关系，并设计了稀疏关系注意力机制，其计算复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量的平方。这从架构层面确保了计算的可扩展性。作者还形式化定义了架构、推导了注意力机制的复杂度，并提供了开源的参考实现。

Result: 在Synthea合成电子健康记录数据集上，使用SNOMED CT条件码的多标签预测任务（具有高分类基数和长尾标签分布的特点）进行评测。与表格、关系和时序图基线方法相比，RHT生成语义更连贯的嵌入且保持计算可扩展性。最佳稀有码召回率由XGBoost取得，而RHT在嵌入语义一致性上最强。消融研究量化了各架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: RHT作为一种统一的关系学习架构，在嵌入质量和计算可扩展性之间取得了良好的平衡，特别适用于高基数、长尾分布的医疗数据场景。然而其在稀有码预测上仍不如XGBoost，且计划在MIMIC-IV上进行进一步的临床验证，表明该工作尚处于初步验证阶段。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [298] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个用于从536篇基于代理建模（ABM）论文中提取模型相关信息的LLM流水线，并与人工系统文献综述（SLR）结果进行比较。GPT-4.1的论文级准确率约为77.95%，GPT-5.0约为81.67%，字段级准确率在32.40%至100%之间，复杂或主观字段可靠性较差。研究发现LLM之间的一致性可作为输出质量的潜在指标：低一致性可能暗示幻觉，高一致性但低准确率则可能指向人工数据中的噪音或错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的进步为自动化研究流程（尤其是系统文献综述SLR）创造了新机遇，但目前缺乏对LLM在实际SLR中性能的系统评估，尤其是在建模与仿真领域。

Method: 开发了一个LLM流水线，从536篇经同行评审的基于代理建模论文中提取模型相关信息，并将提取结果与人工进行的SLR结果进行对比评估，使用GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0两种模型。

Result: GPT-4.1的论文级准确率约为77.95%，GPT-5.0约为81.67%；字段级准确率范围为32.40%至100.00%，复杂或主观字段表现较差。LLM间低一致性可能暗示幻觉，高一致性但低准确率可能指向人工数据集中的噪音或错误。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM在SLR任务中的提示词开发提供了实践洞见，揭示了LLM在建模与仿真领域进行全规模SLR的潜力与局限性，并指出LLM一致性可作为输出质量评估的辅助信号。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [299] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对电信客户流失预测中"高精度模型不可解释导致无法落地于CRM工作流"的问题，在IBM Telco基准上基准测试了四种分类器（LR、RF、XGBoost、LightGBM），Logistic Regression取得最佳AUC-ROC（0.8411），并引入全局SHAP与实例级SHAP/LIME双粒度解释，最终提出四层CRM集成架构，将风险评分和归因向量转化为分层细分与留存行动模板，预计可将流失率降低3.3-5.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信行业客户流失每月约1.9%，造成数十亿美元年收入损失。虽然预测模型能准确标记高风险客户，但高性能的集成及非线性架构因缺乏可解释性而被排斥在CRM工作流之外——留存专员仅凭概率分数无法设计个性化干预方案，因此需要弥合"预测能力"与"可解释行动指导"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在IBM Telco Customer Churn基准（7,043条记录，19个特征，26.5%流失率，训练集用SMOTE平衡至50%）上基准测试四种分类器：Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost和LightGBM，采用5折交叉验证。解释层面提供两种粒度：全局SHAP排序（识别主导信号如tenure、total charges、month-to-month contract）和实例级SHAP/LIME分解。最后提出四层CRM集成架构：风险评分→分层细分→留存行动模板映射→再训练反馈循环。

Result: Logistic Regression取得最强AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM取得最高准确率（78.42%）；四种模型AUC集中在0.831-0.841的0.011窄带内，5折交叉验证显示领先模型统计上基本持平。针对最高风险五分之一客户的定向干预预计可将整体流失率降低3.3-5.3个百分点，每活动周期可节约约19.9万至31.9万美元。

Conclusion: 可解释性与预测性能并非互相排斥——表现最佳的模型与传统线性基准在统计学上无显著差异，而辅以双粒度可解释性输出后，模型可以无缝嵌入一线CRM工作流。该架构通过将黑箱概率转化为可执行的留存行动，实现了从"预测流失"到"驱动挽留决策"的落地闭环。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>


### [300] [EEG-to-Report: An Annotation and Feature-Text Framework for Training Language Models on Clinical EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26153)
*Xuan-The Tran,Le Trung Kien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EEG-to-Report，一个基于浏览器的注释与特征文本框架，将常规EEG审查与AI就绪数据集构建相结合。该框架支持多格式EEG导入、通道标准化、交互式查看器及多模态注释层（文本+转录语音），并为每个标注片段计算标准化特征集（频谱、时域、熵、Hjorth、连通性和棘波相关描述符），存储为便携式JSON格式，生成对齐的特征文本对以监督多模态EEG语言模型。框架还包含自动报告模块，集成卷积网络集成与大型语言模型生成临床叙述草稿供神经科医生审阅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG报告仍然主要依赖人工撰写，耗时且效率低。现有EEG软件生态系统无法生成现代语言模型训练所需的结构化EEG-文本监督信号，且大多数工具包仅专注于可视化或预处理，对生成高质量AI训练数据的工作流支持有限。因此需要一种能够桥接常规EEG临床审查与AI数据集构建的框架。

Method: EEG-to-Report框架包含以下核心组件：(1) 多格式EEG数据导入与通道标准化；(2) 交互式查看器；(3) 多模态注释层，支持文本输入和转录语音笔记；(4) 特征提取引擎，为每个标注片段计算标准化特征集（频谱、时域、熵、Hjorth、连通性、棘波相关描述符）；(5) 便携式JSON模式存储临床描述与特征；(6) 自动报告模块，采用卷积网络集成与大型语言模型相结合生成可编辑的临床叙述草稿。

Result: 通过试点标注实验，作者展示了EEG-to-Report能够简化标注工作流并产生可编辑的草稿报告，验证了该框架在自动化EEG报告系统中的可复用基础性作用，同时生成了对齐的特征文本对数据以支持多模态EEG语言模型的训练。

Conclusion: EEG-to-Report为EEG临床注释与AI数据集构建提供了统一的、可复用的基础框架，弥合了临床工作流与AI模型训练之间的差距，为自动化EEG报告系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) reporting remains largely manual and time-consuming, and current EEG software ecosystems do not produce the structured EEG-text supervision needed for training modern language models. Most toolboxes focus on visualization or preprocessing, providing limited support for workflows that generate high-quality datasets for AI. We introduce EEG-to-Report, a browser-based annotation and feature-text framework that links routine EEG review with the construction of AI-ready datasets. The framework integrates multi-format EEG ingestion, channel standardization, and an interactive viewer with a multimodal annotation layer that combines typed text and transcribed voice notes. For each annotated segment, a feature extraction engine computes a standardized set of spectral, temporal, entropy, Hjorth, connectivity, and spike-related descriptors, stored alongside clinical descriptions in a portable JSON schema. This yields aligned feature-text pairs designed to supervise multimodal EEG-language models. The framework also includes an auto-report module that couples an ensemble of convolutional networks with a large language model to draft clinical narratives for neurologist review. Using pilot annotations, we describe how EEG-to-Report streamlines annotation workflows and produces editable draft reports, providing a reusable foundation for automated EEG reporting systems.

</details>


### [301] [Selection Bias Correction in Retail Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26156)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过400次蒙特卡洛模拟，系统比较了在零售长尾场景下逆概率加权（IPW）与分层方法在通货膨胀估计中的选择偏差校正效果。结果显示，在四种数据生成机制中，分层方法在三种场景下表现更优（中位误差低至0.04pp，甚至在边界错位场景中比IPW有116倍优势）；IPW仅在平滑多项式关系场景下获胜。即使在具有完美结构知识的预言机IPW配置下，在阶跃函数场景中分层仍占优势，这归因于严重违反治疗权重正性假设（Positivity Assumption），而非IPW方法本身的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售情报通常关注高销量热门产品，可能因忽略"长尾"利基产品而导致经济指标（如通胀估计）产生选择偏差。研究旨在通过模拟比较不同偏差校正方法在零售长尾分布中的稳健性，为实际工程选择提供依据。

Method: 采用400次蒙特卡洛重复实验，设计四种数据生成场景（对齐阶跃函数、平滑梯度、错位断点、多项式关系），将五种设定的IPW方法与不同层数的分层方法进行比较。此外还构造了具有完美结构知识的"预言机IPW"作为理论上限参照。

Result: 分层方法在四种场景中的三种取得优势，保持低于0.04个百分点（pp）的中位误差；在边界错位场景中，分层比IPW有116倍优势。IPW结合样条倾向模型在平滑多项式场景中获胜（0.007pp vs 0.013pp）。预言机IPW在阶跃函数场景中误差为6.06pp，而分层仅为0.008pp，原因是选择概率差异极大（90% vs 1%）导致严重违反正性假设。

Conclusion: 在零售长尾分布且存在严重正性违反（positivity violation）的情况下，分层方法是更安全的工程选择。IPW的局限性主要源于其理论前提（正性假设）被破坏，而非方法论本身存在缺陷，因此方法选择应依赖具体的数据生成机制。

Abstract: Retail intelligence often relies on monitoring popular, high-velocity products, potentially biasing economic indicators by ignoring the "long tail" of niche items. This simulation study investigates selection bias in inflation estimation and compares correction methods across diverse data-generating processes. Through 400 Monte Carlo replications spanning four scenarios--aligned step functions, smooth gradients, misaligned breaks, and polynomial relationships--we test the robustness of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) with five specifications against stratification with varying strata counts. Our findings reveal fundamental limits of weighting methods in retail long-tail contexts: stratification achieves superior performance in three of four scenarios, maintaining sub-0.04pp median error even when boundaries deliberately misalign with population breaks (116x advantage over IPW). However, IPW with spline propensity models wins under smooth polynomial relationships (median error 0.007pp vs. 0.013pp), demonstrating context-dependency. Critically, even an oracle IPW specification with perfect structural knowledge achieves 6.06pp error compared to stratification's 0.008pp in step-function scenarios. This reflects violation of the Positivity Assumption--a fundamental causal inference requirement--rather than IPW methodological inferiority. When selection probabilities differ dramatically (90% vs. 1%), weighting methods operate outside their theoretical design envelope. These results demonstrate that stratification provides a safer engineering choice in retail long-tail distributions with severe positivity violations.

</details>


### [302] [GROUND: Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-Based Enterprise Analytics Through Governed Semantic Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26157)
*Aravind Sasidharan Pillai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROUND框架，通过在LLM生成的文本到SQL分析流程中强制约束一个受治理的语义层（包含已批准的指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器和行级安全），显著减少幻觉和违规行为，并在合成基准和真实NHTSA数据上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据仓库的自然语言分析在生产环境中受到幻觉指标、无效连接、错误粒度、不安全数据访问和不支持的语义等问题的限制。现有的文本到SQL系统仅依赖数据库模式或检索文档，缺乏企业报告所需的受治理业务语义（批准的指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器及行级安全性），导致无法满足生产级安全性和正确性要求。

Method: GROUND（Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions）框架包含三个核心机制：（1）提供经批准的语义定义；（2）将用户意图绑定到受治理的指标和维度；（3）在执行前按照模式、指标、连接、粒度、过滤器、安全和成本规则验证生成的SQL，违规时进行重试或放弃回答。

Result: 在100个合成企业报告基准问题上，GROUND是唯一在所有六个评估类别中无测量幻觉的系统，而未经治理的系统在多道问题上违反行级安全。仅有精确指标定义但无访问策略的语义条件仍会泄露数据，表明治理不能仅靠指标保真度取代。在真实NHTSA车辆安全数据上的独立手工标注实验及跨四模型三提供商的对抗测试中，GROUND的强制保证（尤其过滤器和行级安全）在所有模型上零违规，而判断依赖的行为（如拒绝未定义指标）仍可能出错。

Conclusion: GROUND证明了将LLM生成的SQL分析约束在受治理语义层中，可以有效消除幻觉指标和安全违规，特别是在过滤器和行级安全方面提供零违规的强保证。然而，依赖模型判断的软性行为仍有改进空间，治理不能仅依赖单一机制如指标保真度，需要多层次的强制规则共同保障。

Abstract: Natural-language analytics over enterprise data warehouses is increasingly important, but production use is limited by hallucinated metrics, invalid joins, wrong grain, unsafe data access, and unsupported explanations. Existing text-to-SQL systems often ground generation in database schemas or retrieved documentation, while enterprise reporting also requires governed business semantics: approved metrics, dimensions, join paths, filters, and row-level security. This paper introduces GROUND, Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions, a framework that constrains LLM-generated analytics to a governed semantic layer. GROUND supplies approved definitions, binds user intent to governed metrics and dimensions, and validates generated SQL against schema, metric, join, grain, filter, security, and cost rules before execution. On violations, it retries or abstains.
  In a 100-question synthetic enterprise-reporting benchmark, GROUND is compared with direct schema-only text-to-SQL, schema-RAG, and semantic-only grounding under one shared model. GROUND is the only system free of measured hallucinations across all six evaluated categories, while ungoverned systems violate row-level security on many questions. A semantic-only condition with exact metric definitions but no access policy still leaks data, showing that governance cannot be replaced by metric fidelity alone. The findings are replicated on real U.S. NHTSA vehicle-safety data with independent hand-authored gold and tested on an adversarial set across four models from three providers. GROUND's enforced guarantees, especially filters and row-level security, hold with zero violations on every model, while judgment-dependent behaviors such as refusing undefined metrics remain fallible.

</details>


### [303] [SAREF-based Ontology for Distributed AI Workflows across the Edge-Fog-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26160)
*Viorica Rozina Chifu,Tudor Cioara,Vasile Ofrim,Liana Toderean,Ionut Anghel,Laura Daniele,Cornelis Bouter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种符合SAREF标准的本体，用于在边缘-雾-云连续体中表示分布式AI工作流，通过扩展SAREF4SYST本体建立统一语义模型，支持AI工作流与异构计算基础设施的语义互操作、自动化推理和资源感知编排。实验在智能电网场景中验证，部署成功率达90-100%，平均编排决策时间低于80毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义模型对跨异构边缘、雾、云环境中分布式AI工作流及其执行的表示支持有限，导致AI流程和资源常使用不兼容的语义表示，影响互操作性、编排和复用。

Method: 扩展SAREF4SYST本体，引入AI流水线、可执行AI作业、计算资源、部署约束和通信关系等概念，构建统一语义模型，并通过SPARQL查询和语义推理验证能力问题，以智能电网能源服务编排场景作为概念验证。

Result: 所有能力问题均通过SPARQL查询和语义推理成功验证；实验部署成功率达90-100%，平均编排决策时间低于80毫秒，证明其在异构边缘-雾-云环境中确保分布式AI编排语义互操作性的有效性。

Conclusion: 该本体与ETSI SAREF生态完全对齐，能够有效支撑异构边缘-雾-云环境中AI工作流的部署、执行推理和工作负载自适应，为分布式AI编排提供统一的语义互操作基础。

Abstract: Nowadays semantic models provide limited support for representing distributed AI workflows and their execution across heterogeneous edge, fog, and cloud environments. Therefore, AI processes and resources are often described using incompatible semantic representations, affecting the interoperability, orchestration, and reuse.
